# Banner replacement implementation (client delivery of 16 Oct 2026, second set)

Date: 19 Oct 2026. Branch `develop`. The asset and manifest changes below were committed by the user as `130b85c` ("Added optimized images", 108 files: 107 cut-out adds/removals + `banner-art.json`) on top of f36c306 / 8635b34. Source: `~/Downloads/Cambridge Hospital - Website Images` (untouched). Audit: `docs/banner-assets-audit.md` (with its 19 Oct erratum) and `docs/banner-assets-inventory.csv`. This session committed and deployed nothing.

**Concurrent work, not from this session:** at 14:06-14:07 another session wrote `src/assets/regions/sa/careers/banner-cutout.webp` and `src/assets/regions/sa/international/banner-cutout.webp` (replacing the KSA `.png` files) and changed the `sa:careers/banner` / `sa:international/banner` entries in `banner-art.json` to the Global geometry. Both banners are on this session's reject list (edge artefacts in its own extraction); that uncommitted work was left untouched and is not covered by this report.

## A. Executive summary

- Assets replaced or added: **73** cut-outs (19 new regional overrides, 54 replacements of existing files) + 19 new `banner-art.json` entries for the new overrides (copies of the Global geometry; Conditions gets its own). No existing entry changed.
- Replacements visually verified (before/after render at the live geometry + Chrome hero screenshots): **73** of 73.
- Chrome responsive pass (11 widths, every affected route, EN + AR): **71** of 73 assets passed with no geometry change, no horizontal scroll, no console/network error; the rest are listed in section B.
- Actual Safari (macOS) testing: **0 passed, 0 failed, 73 pending** - no Mac, iPhone or WebKit build is available on this Linux machine (see E).
- Deferred: **45** client files / destinations (section D): regional pairing, known defects, Global = UAE confirmation, duplicates of a processed file, and extraction results below the acceptance threshold.

**How the composition was preserved.** The site draws each hero from one transparent people cut-out (`<key>-cutout.*`) at the size and spot in `src/data/banner-art.json` over a CSS gradient and the shared pill SVG. No geometry, CSS, page content or mapping logic was changed. Each new cut-out was built by aligning the current cut-out (its picture and silhouette) onto the client composite by normalised cross-correlation (scale + translation, feet on the frame bottom), taking the composite's pixels at that spot at up to 2x of the display size, keying the alpha against the per-row gradient (thin pill strokes removed) and bounding it by the current silhouette. Acceptance: NCC >= 0.88 and silhouette IoU >= 0.96 (buildings: NCC >= 0.80 with IoU >= 0.985), or explicit approval after a 1:1 visual check; every result was reviewed at 1:1 and nine with residual edge artefacts were rejected (section D). Inside the pills.svg geometry (placed at the composite stack spot) the current cut-out stroke-free silhouette bounds the key, so no pill outline is baked into a cut-out. The pure-Pillow extractor lives in the session scratchpad (OpenCV, which `tools/banners/composite.py` needs, is not installed); it reproduces the project pipeline's conventions (lossless WebP, one file per key/region, old extension removed).

## E. Build and validation results

- **Build:** npx astro build (outDir .astro/qc-banners/after): 1666 pages, exit 0; only the pre-existing INEFFECTIVE_DYNAMIC_IMPORT Vite warnings (also in the before build).
- **Lint / type check:** not configured in package.json (no lint or astro check script); not run.
- **Static export:** the build is the static export (output: static); the after build was served with tools/qc/serve.mjs and every changed route loaded.
- **Automated tests:** npm test: all passed before and after the change (functions, filters, conditions, sanitize, news, calculators, client-096-098).
- **Asset-path validation:** every changed hero loaded its new image in the after build (2376 route x width shots, 0 not loaded); the build resolves every cut-out through img() at build time, so a missing path would fail the build.
- **Regional / language validation:** UAE-only overrides (regions/ae) and KSA files (regions/sa) are served only on /ae and /sa; Global pages render the unchanged Global assets except the shared files that were already sourced from the UAE files (care heroes, hospital buildings). Each route was shot in EN and AR.
- **Chrome testing:** Google Chrome 155 (playwright-core), 11 widths (320-1920), 2376 shots on 216 route/edition/language combinations: 2332 of 2332 shots of the 69 replaced assets show no geometry change (hero, people box and pill stack within 2 px of before), median pixel difference 0.4%; horizontal scroll 0; console/network errors none except the pre-existing missing Google Maps key on Contact. Conditions & Specialities (new picture, new geometry) changes by design: people box moves, the hero grows 10-29 px on phones and ~120 px at 768 (taller picture under the copy); checked by eye at 360/768/1024/1440, EN + AR, no overlap or clipping.
- **Actual Safari desktop testing:** NOT DONE: no macOS machine available; status Safari verification pending for every banner.
- **Actual iPhone Safari testing:** NOT DONE: no iPhone available; status Safari verification pending.
- **Unavailable environments:** macOS Safari, iPhone Safari, Playwright WebKit (not installed). Before/after screenshots: .astro/qc-banners/shots-before and shots-after; comparison: session scratchpad compare/compare.json.

1. Whether Global should use the UAE images (then the `ae:` overrides added here become the shared assets and the Global pages get the same sharper banners).
2. ~~Live subject/pill size vs the larger composition~~ - resolved: the delivery uses the existing geometry (audit erratum, 19 Oct).
3. Correct KSA files for Who We Are, Inpatient Care, Long-Term Care & Rehabilitation hub and Your Opinion Matters (the delivered ones are the UAE/Global pictures).
4. The changed KSA Patient Testimonials picture (new mother) and the UAE/Global Long-Term Acute Care pairing (delivered as the KSA pairing, bed removed).
5. Global and UAE Your Opinion Matters files.
6. Which page the two "Knowledge Centre" files are for.
7. A wider Insurance original, or approval of the hard desk/chair edge (also whether the Speech & Language desk and the Musculoskeletal bar ends may stay cut flat, as on the live site today).
8. Condition-page originals for Bugs 195/197 (Multiple Sclerosis, Acquired Brain Injury, Parkinson's, Stroke condition, Neurorehabilitation treatment bed).

## G. Rollback procedure

Everything is in commit `130b85c`. To undo all of it: `git revert 130b85c` (one commit, assets + manifest only; it does not touch the other developer's changes). To restore single banners, check out the previous files from the parent commit, e.g. `git checkout 130b85c~1 -- <previous path>`, delete the new path, and for a new override remove its `banner-art.json` entry. Per banner:

| Destination | Restore |
|---|---|
| `ae:about/banner` | delete `src/assets/regions/ae/about/banner-cutout.webp` and remove the `ae:about/banner` entry from `src/data/banner-art.json` (`git checkout 130b85c~1 -- src/data/banner-art.json` restores the whole manifest) |
| `ae:accreditations/banner` | delete `src/assets/regions/ae/accreditations/banner-cutout.webp` and remove the `ae:accreditations/banner` entry from `src/data/banner-art.json` (`git checkout 130b85c~1 -- src/data/banner-art.json` restores the whole manifest) |
| `ae:careers/banner` | delete `src/assets/regions/ae/careers/banner-cutout.webp` and remove the `ae:careers/banner` entry from `src/data/banner-art.json` (`git checkout 130b85c~1 -- src/data/banner-art.json` restores the whole manifest) |
| `ae:conditions/banner` | delete `src/assets/regions/ae/conditions/banner-cutout.webp` and remove the `ae:conditions/banner` entry from `src/data/banner-art.json` (`git checkout 130b85c~1 -- src/data/banner-art.json` restores the whole manifest) |
| `ae:contact-page/banner` | delete `src/assets/regions/ae/contact-page/banner-cutout.webp` and remove the `ae:contact-page/banner` entry from `src/data/banner-art.json` (`git checkout 130b85c~1 -- src/data/banner-art.json` restores the whole manifest) |
| `ae:doctors-page/banner` | delete `src/assets/regions/ae/doctors-page/banner-cutout.webp` and remove the `ae:doctors-page/banner` entry from `src/data/banner-art.json` (`git checkout 130b85c~1 -- src/data/banner-art.json` restores the whole manifest) |
